# Phase 6b cohort tables - Within 54-250: no reading < 54 and none > 250 (N = 890)

Back to the [cohort index](research_report_05_glucose_cohort_tables.md). Per outcome: (A) model output on the raw scale, one row per single-predictor model; (B) effect per 1 SD, BH q (rule and informational), adjusted R²/AUC, AIC differences, CV. Full term-by-term output: `model_output_tables/phase6b/`.
